<body lang=3DEN-US link=3D"#0563C1" vlink=3D"#954F72" style=3D'tab-interval=
:.5in;
word-wrap:break-word'>

<div class=3DWordSection1>

<p class=3DMsoNormal>This is a comprehensive community audit for <b>Palo Al=
to
Networks (PANW)</b>.<o:p></o:p></p>

<p class=3DMsoNormal>Palo Alto Networks is the dominant &quot;platform&quot;
player in cybersecurity, spanning network security (Strata), cloud security
(Prisma), and security operations (Cortex). Because their products are often
mission-critical and complex, the discourse is highly technical, focusing on
configuration, troubleshooting, and architectural design.<o:p></o:p></p>

<div class=3DMsoNormal align=3Dcenter style=3D'text-align:center'>

<hr size=3D2 width=3D"100%" align=3Dcenter>

</div>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><b>1. Company Analysis &amp; Metadata<o:p></o:p></b></=
p>

<ul style=3D'margin-top:0in' type=3Ddisc>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l0 level1 lfo1;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Company
     Name:</b> Palo Alto Networks<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l0 level1 lfo1;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Product
     Category:</b> Cybersecurity (Platform)<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l0 level1 lfo1;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Core
     Products:</b> Next-Generation Firewalls (NGFW), Prisma Cloud (CNAPP),
     Cortex XDR/XSOAR, <span class=3DSpellE>GlobalProtect</span> (VPN/SASE)=
, Unit
     42 (Threat Intel).<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l0 level1 lfo1;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Target
     Customer:</b> Enterprise IT, Global 2000, Government/Federal, MSSPs
     (Managed Security Service Providers).<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l0 level1 lfo1;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Target
     Audience:</b> Network Security Engineers, CISOs, Cloud Architects, SOC
     Analysts, <span class=3DSpellE>DevSecOps</span> Engineers.<o:p></o:p><=
/li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l0 level1 lfo1;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>SEO/Meta
     Themes:</b> Zero Trust, SASE, AI-Powered SOC, Cloud Native Security,
     Threat Intelligence, Network Transformation.<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l0 level1 lfo1;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Competitive
     Landscape:</b> Fortinet, Check Point, Cisco, Zscaler, CrowdStrike, Wiz
     (for Prisma Cloud), Netskope.<o:p></o:p></li>
</ul>

<div class=3DMsoNormal align=3Dcenter style=3D'text-align:center'>

<hr size=3D2 width=3D"100%" align=3Dcenter>

</div>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><b>2. Discipline Mapping &amp; Audience Type<o:p></o:p=
></b></p>

<table class=3DMsoNormalTable border=3D0 cellpadding=3D0 style=3D'mso-cells=
pacing:1.5pt;
 mso-yfti-tbllook:1184'>
 <tr style=3D'mso-yfti-irow:0;mso-yfti-firstrow:yes'>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Discipline<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Audience Type<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Explanation<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr style=3D'mso-yfti-irow:1'>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal><b>Network Engineering</b><o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>End-user Practitioners<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Pros managing physical/virtual firewalls, routing, a=
nd <span
  class=3DSpellE>GlobalProtect</span> VPNs.<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr style=3D'mso-yfti-irow:2'>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal><b>Cloud Security (CNAPP)</b><o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Cloud Architects / DevOps<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Users securing AWS/Azure/GCP workloads via Prisma Cl=
oud;
  focus on shift-left.<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr style=3D'mso-yfti-irow:3'>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal><b>Security Operations (SecOps)</b><o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>SOC Analysts / IR<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Users of Cortex XDR and XSOAR for incident response =
and
  automation.<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr style=3D'mso-yfti-irow:4'>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal><b>Executive Leadership</b><o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Buyers / CISOs<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Decision-makers focused on ROI, platform consolidati=
on,
  and risk posture.<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr style=3D'mso-yfti-irow:5'>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal><b>Automation / DevOps</b><o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Developers<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Building integrations via PAN-OS APIs, Terraform
  providers, and Ansible.<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
 <tr style=3D'mso-yfti-irow:6;mso-yfti-lastrow:yes'>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal><b>Threat Research</b><o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Analysts<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td style=3D'padding:.75pt .75pt .75pt .75pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal>Following Unit 42 reports to update local security
  policies and IOCs.<o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
</table>

<div class=3DMsoNormal align=3Dcenter style=3D'text-align:center'>

<hr size=3D2 width=3D"100%" align=3Dcenter>

</div>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><b>3. Community Intelligence Map (50+ Communities)<o:p=
></o:p></b></p>

<p class=3DMsoNormal>This list prioritizes spaces where candid
&quot;peer-to-peer&quot; help and vendor critiques occur.<o:p></o:p></p>

<table class=3DMsoTableGrid border=3D1 cellspacing=3D0 cellpadding=3D0

  href=3D"https://www.google.com/url?sa=3DE&amp;q=3Dhttps%3A%2F%2Ftwitter.c=
om%2FUnit42_Intel"
  target=3D"_blank">@Unit42_Intel</a><o:p></o:p></p>
  </td>
  <td valign=3Dtop style=3D'border-top:none;border-left:none;border-bottom:=
solid #BFBFBF 1.0pt;
  mso-border-bottom-themecolor:background1;mso-border-bottom-themeshade:191;
  border-right:solid #BFBFBF 1.0pt;mso-border-right-themecolor:background1;
  mso-border-right-themeshade:191;mso-border-top-alt:solid #BFBFBF .5pt;
  mso-border-top-themecolor:background1;mso-border-top-themeshade:191;
  mso-border-left-alt:solid #BFBFBF .5pt;mso-border-left-themecolor:backgro=
und1;
  mso-border-left-themeshade:191;mso-border-alt:solid #BFBFBF .5pt;mso-bord=
er-themecolor:
  background1;mso-border-themeshade:191;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
  <p class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'margin-bottom:8.0pt;line-height:115%'>Though
  official, their researchers (e.g., Jen Miller-Osborn) are influencers.<o:=
p></o:p></p>
  </td>
 </tr>
</table>

<div class=3DMsoNormal align=3Dcenter style=3D'text-align:center'>

<hr size=3D2 width=3D"100%" align=3Dcenter>

</div>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><b>5. Strategic Insights<o:p></o:p></b></p>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><b>1. Top communities where real conversations happen<=
o:p></o:p></b></p>

<p class=3DMsoNormal>The <b>r/<span class=3DSpellE>PaloAltoNetworks</span><=
/b>
subreddit is the undisputed king of peer-to-peer technical help. Unlike the
official &quot;Live Community,&quot; users here will openly tell you to avo=
id a
certain OS release due to memory leaks or UI bugs. <span class=3DSpellE><b>=
MacAdmins</b></span><b>
Slack</b> is also critical for real-world VPN (<span class=3DSpellE>GlobalP=
rotect</span>)
deployment issues.<o:p></o:p></p>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><b>2. Hidden / Unexpected communities<o:p></o:p></b></=
p>

<ul style=3D'margin-top:0in' type=3Ddisc>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l1 level1 lfo2;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>The
     MSP Subreddit (r/MSP):</b> This is where &quot;<span class=3DSpellE>Pl=
atformization</span>&quot;
     is tested. MSPs hate complex licensing. If you want to know if PANW's
     pricing is actually working, listen to MSP owners complaining about th=
eir
     margins.<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l1 level1 lfo2;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><span
     class=3DSpellE><b>HomeLab</b></span><b>/<span class=3DSpellE>OpenWrt</=
span>:</b>
     There is a massive underground of engineers trying to run virtual PAN-=
OS
     at home to keep their certifications current. Their feedback on
     &quot;usability&quot; is often 2 years ahead of enterprise trends.<o:p=
></o:p></li>
</ul>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><b>3. Narrative Opportunities (Gaps &amp; Complaints)<=
o:p></o:p></b></p>

<ul style=3D'margin-top:0in' type=3Ddisc>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l2 level1 lfo3;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Licensing
     Fatigue:</b> A recurring theme is the complexity of PANW licensing
     (credits, bundles, add-ons). There is a narrative gap for
     &quot;Simplification.&quot;<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l2 level1 lfo3;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>The
     &quot;Wiz&quot; Effect:</b> In the Cloud Security space, Prisma Cloud =
is
     often criticized for being a &quot;franken-product&quot; (collection of
     acquisitions). Peer-to-peer discussions favor more cohesive, &quot;bor=
n in
     the cloud&quot; UIs.<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l2 level1 lfo3;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Support
     Response Times:</b> Users frequently complain that Tier 1 support is
     declining. This creates an opportunity for &quot;Expert-led&quot; cont=
ent
     or third-party professional services.<o:p></o:p></li>
</ul>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><b>4. Community Engagement Strategy<o:p></o:p></b></p>

<ul style=3D'margin-top:0in' type=3Ddisc>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l3 level1 lfo4;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Where
     to Participate:</b> Engineers should be active in <b>r/<span class=3DS=
pellE>PaloAltoNetworks</span></b>
     and <b>r/Networking</b>, not to sell, but to provide
     &quot;Unofficial&quot; configuration tips.<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l3 level1 lfo4;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Where
     Founders/Execs should post:</b> <b>LinkedIn</b> is the space for the
     &quot;<span class=3DSpellE>Platformization</span>&quot; narrative, but=
 <b>X
     (Twitter)</b> is where they must engage with security researchers (like
     Kevin Beaumont) to defend their security posture during CVE disclosure=
s.<o:p></o:p></li>
 <li class=3DMsoNormal style=3D'mso-list:l3 level1 lfo4;tab-stops:list .5in=
'><b>Thought
     Leadership:</b> Focus on <b>Packet Pushers</b> (Podcasts) and <b>Mediu=
m/Substack</b>.
     The audience wants architectural &quot;How-To&quot; guides that solve
     multi-vendor problems, not just PANW-exclusive sales pitches.<o:p></o:=
p></li>
</ul>

<p class=3DMsoNormal><o:p>&nbsp;</o:p></p>

</div>

</body>

</html>
